# Test source

```ts
  1  | import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
  2  | import { mountApiMocks, setSessionState } from "./support/mockApi";
  3  | 
  4  | test.describe("Performance budgets", () => {
  5  |   test("tab transition remains under budget on large run payload", async ({ page }) => {
  6  |     await mountApiMocks(page);
  7  |     await setSessionState(page, {
  8  |       page: "structured_analysis",
  9  |       structuredRunId: "run_struct_001",
  10 |       collateralRiskSettings: {
  11 |         productFamily: "PRIME_JUMBO",
  12 |         mode: "none",
  13 |       },
  14 |     });
  15 |     await page.goto("/");
  16 | 
  17 |     const start = Date.now();
  18 |     await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Waterfall + Triggers" }).click();
  19 |     await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Bond Cashflows" }).click();
  20 |     await expect(page.getByText("Data Preview")).toBeVisible();
  21 |     const elapsedMs = Date.now() - start;
  22 | 
  23 |     // Quality ratchet for structurer productivity: do not loosen this
  24 |     // threshold to make CI pass. Fix the underlying UI performance instead.
> 25 |     expect(elapsedMs).toBeLessThan(2500);
     |                       ^ Error: expect(received).toBeLessThan(expected)
  26 |   });
  27 | });
  28 |
```
